| `MM_ToxicBite.description` | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Des toxines circulent dans le sang. Elles peuvent provenir de diverses sources, notamment des toxines environnementales, des morsures venimeuses ou des armes empoisonnées.<br><br>À forte dose, l'intoxication est mortelle. Même à faible dose, elle peut provoquer des cancers.<br><br>Contrairement à l'intoxication du jeu de base, ces dégâts toxiques ne rendent pas impropres à la consommation les créatures qui en meurent. |  |

| `MM_Ahuizotl.label` | ahuizotl | ahuizotl | ahuizotl |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.description` | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | L'ahuizotl est une créature légendaire de la mythologie aztèque. Il ressemble à un chien dont la fourrure imperméable forme souvent des pointes. Ses mains lui permettent de manipuler des objets, et une main supplémentaire termine sa queue. On le redoute pour son goût pour la chair humaine, en particulier les ongles, les yeux et les dents. Il vivrait dans l'eau ou à proximité et utiliserait sa main caudale pour saisir ses proies et les entraîner dans les profondeurs afin de les noyer.<br><br>Les ahuizotls ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les ahuizotls saisissent habilement leurs proies avec la main au bout de leur queue et les lacèrent de leurs griffes. Cela rend la retraite difficile au corps à corps. |  |

| `MM_Basilisk.description` | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | Dans les bestiaires européens, le basilic est un reptile légendaire, réputé roi des serpents et capable de tuer d'un seul regard. Pline l'Ancien le décrivait comme un serpent portant sur la tête une tache blanche semblable à un diadème. On lui attribuait un effet extrêmement néfaste sur la végétation : toutes les plantes autour de lui dépérissaient et pourrissaient.<br><br>Les basilics ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : le basilic est une bête particulièrement déplaisante. Son regard peut pétrifier ses victimes, tandis que l'herbe et les buissons alentour pourrissent lentement et se dessèchent, le nourrissant au passage. Heureusement, les basilics sont peu communs. |  |

| `MM_Catoblepas.description` | The catoblepas is a legendary creature from Ethiopia, said to have the body of a cape buffalo and an oversized head that is constantly pointing downwards due to its great weight.<br><br>Catoblepas aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The stare of the catoblepas can turn people into stone. It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | The catoblepas is a legendary creature from Ethiopia, said to have the body of a cape buffalo and an oversized head that is constantly pointing downwards due to its great weight.<br><br>Catoblepas aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The stare of the catoblepas can turn people into stone. It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | Le catoblépas est une créature légendaire d'Éthiopie, dotée d'un corps de buffle du Cap et d'une tête démesurée que son poids maintient constamment tournée vers le sol.<br><br>Les catoblépas ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : le regard du catoblépas peut transformer les gens en pierre. Bien qu'il ne soit pas naturellement agressif, ce pouvoir dangereux le protège de tout prédateur naturel. |  |

| `MM_Cerberus.description` | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | Dans la mythologie grecque, Cerbère, souvent appelé le chien d'Hadès, est un chien à trois têtes qui garde les portes des Enfers pour empêcher les morts d'en sortir. Fils des monstres Échidna et Typhon, il est surtout connu pour sa capture par Héraclès, l'un des douze travaux du héros.<br><br>Les cerbères ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les cerbères sont plus difficiles à dresser et moins intelligents que les chiens ordinaires. Leurs trois têtes en font toutefois des animaux de combat TRÈS dangereux, surtout lorsque leurs mâchoires sont modifiées par des implants bioniques. |  |

| `MM_CeryneianHind.description` | In Greek mythology, the Ceryneian Hind was a mythical creature that lived in Ceryneia, Greece, and took the form of an enormous deer, larger than a bull, with golden antlers like a stag. To bring it back alive to Eurystheus in Mycenae was the third labour of Heracles.<br><br>Ceryneian Hinds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Both Ceryneian Hinds and Harts are very fast creatures. They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Ceryneian Hinds produce milk. | In Greek mythology, the Ceryneian Hind was a mythical creature that lived in Ceryneia, Greece, and took the form of an enormous deer, larger than a bull, with golden antlers like a stag. To bring it back alive to Eurystheus in Mycenae was the third labour of Heracles.<br><br>Ceryneian Hinds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Both Ceryneian Hinds and Harts are very fast creatures. They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Ceryneian Hinds produce milk. | Dans la mythologie grecque, la biche de Cérynie vivait à Cérynie, en Grèce. | `MM_ErymanthianBoar.description` | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | Le sanglier d'Érymanthe était un porc sauvage monstrueux et gigantesque qui habitait les pentes du mont Érymanthe et ravageait parfois les champs des paysans. Sa capture constituait le quatrième travail d'Héraclès. Pour y parvenir, le héros le poussa dans une neige épaisse qui ralentit considérablement sa progression et annula son avantage.<br><br>Les sangliers d'Érymanthe ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les défenses du sanglier d'Érymanthe peuvent infliger des plaies graves qui continuent de saigner et d'occasionner d'autres blessures, le rendant TRÈS dangereux au corps à corps. |  |

| `MM_Fenghuang.label` | fenghuang | fenghuang | fenghuang |  |
| `MM_Fenghuang.description` | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | Le fenghuang est un oiseau légendaire des mythologies d'Asie orientale, symbole de grande vertu et de grâce. Il représente aussi l'union du yin et du yang. Selon la croyance, il n'apparaît que dans les lieux bénis par une paix, une prospérité ou un bonheur exceptionnels. Traditionnellement décrit comme un assemblage de nombreux oiseaux, il est souvent appelé « phénix chinois », bien que ses ressemblances avec le phénix occidental soient superficielles.<br><br>Les fenghuangs ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les fenghuangs apprivoisés dégagent une aura de paix et de sérénité qui remonte le moral des personnes proches. |  |

| `MM_Griffin.description` | The griffin is a creature with the body, tail, and back legs of a lion; the head and wings of an eagle; and an eagle's talons as its front feet. Griffins are especially powerful and majestic creatures, known for guarding treasure and priceless possessions, and valued as trusted steeds.<br><br>Griffins aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Griffins are excellent mounts, receiving bonuses to speed and armour when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The griffin is a creature with the body, tail, and back legs of a lion; the head and wings of an eagle; and an eagle's talons as its front feet. Griffins are especially powerful and majestic creatures, known for guarding treasure and priceless possessions, and valued as trusted steeds.<br><br>Griffins aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Griffins are excellent mounts, receiving bonuses to speed and armour when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | Le griffon possède le corps, la queue et les pattes arrière d'un lion, la tête et les ailes d'un aigle, ainsi que des serres d'aigle à l'avant. Puissants et majestueux, les griffons sont connus comme gardiens de trésors et de biens précieux, et appréciés comme montures fiables.<br><br>Les griffons ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les griffons sont d'excellentes montures et bénéficient de bonus de vitesse et d'armure lorsqu'ils sont montés. Cette capacité nécessite le mod Giddy-Up 2 - Continued (Hue, coco 2, repris). |  |

| `MM_Ieltxu.description` | The ieltxu is a genie bird in Basque mythology. Usually depicted as a fire-breathing, pitch-black raven, the ieltxu is not a malicious creature, though it is often described as mischievous. Ieltxu are creatures of the night, usually appearing in dark forests under the cover of darkness.<br><br>Ieltxu aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Ieltxu can breathe fire when threatened. They are nocturnal creatures that sleep during the day. | The ieltxu is a genie bird in Basque mythology. Usually depicted as a fire-breathing, pitch-black raven, the ieltxu is not a malicious creature, though it is often described as mischievous. Ieltxu are creatures of the night, usually appearing in dark forests under the cover of darkness.<br><br>Ieltxu aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Ieltxu can breathe fire when threatened. They are nocturnal creatures that sleep during the day. | L'ieltxu est un oiseau-esprit de la mythologie basque. Généralement représenté comme un corbeau noir de jais crachant du feu, il n'est pas malveillant, bien qu'on le décrive souvent comme espiègle. C'est une créature nocturne qui apparaît habituellement dans les forêts obscures, à la faveur de la nuit.<br><br>Les ieltxus ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les ieltxus peuvent cracher du feu lorsqu'ils sont menacés. Ce sont des créatures nocturnes qui dorment pendant la journée. |  |

| `MM_Kappa.label` | kappa | kappa | kappa |  |
| `MM_Kappa.description` | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | Le kappa est un esprit des rivières du folklore japonais. Il ressemble à un humanoïde vert aux mains et aux pieds palmés, portant une carapace de tortue sur le dos. Une cavité sur sa tête, son « assiette », contient un peu d'eau ; si cette eau se renverse ou s'évapore, le kappa s'affaiblit considérablement. Souvent espiègles, les kappas ne sont pas foncièrement malveillants. Une fois apprivoisés, ils peuvent accomplir toutes sortes de tâches pour les humains, comme aider les fermiers à irriguer leurs terres.<br><br>Les kappas ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les kappas sont peureux et fuient le combat s'ils perdent trop de santé. Correctement dressés, avec au moins le dressage de garde, ils peuvent aider les colons à récolter. |  |

| `MM_Kitsune.description` | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | Le kitsune est une créature légendaire du folklore japonais, semblable à un renard très intelligent. Ses pouvoirs paranormaux augmentent avec son âge et sa sagesse. Il peut posséder jusqu'à neuf queues ; en général, plus elles sont nombreuses, plus le kitsune est âgé et puissant.<br><br>Les kitsunes ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les kitsunes apprivoisés régénèrent lentement les blessures des colons et des animaux proches. |  |

| `MM_LernaeanHydra.label` | lernaean hydra | lernaean hydra | hydre de Lerne |  |
| `MM_LernaeanHydra.description` | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | L'hydre de Lerne est un monstre aquatique serpentin des mythologies grecque et romaine. Son repaire était le lac de Lerne, en Argolide, d'où son nom. Son souffle et son sang sont extrêmement venimeux, et elle possède d'impressionnantes capacités de régénération. La tuer constituait le deuxième travail d'Héraclès.<br><br>Les hydres ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Elles ont été créées dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les hydres régénèrent leurs blessures assez rapidement. Dangereuses au corps à corps, elles sont surtout redoutables par leur souffle acide empoisonné. Heureusement, elles sont plutôt craintives et s'approchent rarement des humains. |  |

| `MM_LesserWyvern.label` | lesser wyvern | lesser wyvern | petite wyverne |  |
| `MM_LesserWyvern.description` | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | Dans l'héraldique européenne médiévale, la wyverne est un dragon bipède ailé figurant sur de nombreux étendards et blasons. Contrairement au dragon à quatre pattes, elle crache rarement du feu dans l'héraldique et le folklore. Elle est plutôt associée au poison, par ses crocs venimeux ou sa dangereuse queue barbelée.<br><br>Les petites wyvernes ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Elles ont été créées dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les petites wyvernes sont extrêmement venimeuses, résistantes aux dégâts et presque insensibles à la douleur extrême. Heureusement, elles sont très rares. Contrairement aux grandes wyvernes, elles ne peuvent pas cracher de poison. Les grandes wyvernes apparaissent dans le mod Witcher Monster Hunt (La Traque du Sorceleur). |  |

| `MM_Manticore.label` | manticore | manticore | manticore |  |
| `MM_Manticore.description` | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | La manticore est une créature monstrueuse de la mythologie perse. Elle possède une tête mi-humaine, mi-léonine, un corps de lion et une queue de scorpion garnie d'épines venimeuses semblables à des piquants de porc-épic. Cette manticore a aussi été modifiée pour porter des ailes de chauve-souris non fonctionnelles, absentes des représentations historiques. C'est un féroce prédateur capable de traquer ses proies et de les attaquer à distance.<br><br>Les manticores ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Elles ont été créées dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les manticores peuvent projeter des aiguillons empoisonnés depuis leur queue de scorpion. |  |

| `MM_Pegasus.description` | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | Le pégase est un cheval divin ailé, parmi les créatures les plus célèbres de la mythologie grecque. Les pégases sont des chevaux blancs portant une paire d'ailes emplumées sur le dos. Les récits leur prêtaient la faculté de voler, mais ces créatures semblent n'avoir des ailes que pour l'apparence : aucune ingénierie génétique ne pourrait permettre à des ailes aussi frêles de soutenir leur poids. Ils sont appréciés comme montures rapides et fiables.<br><br>Les pégases ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les pégases sont d'excellentes montures et bénéficient d'un important bonus de vitesse lorsqu'ils sont montés. Cette capacité nécessite le mod Giddy-Up 2 - Continued (Hue, coco 2, repris). |  |

| `MM_Phoenix.label` | phoenix | phoenix | phénix |  |
| `MM_Phoenix.description` | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | Dans la mythologie égyptienne antique, puis grecque et romaine, le phénix est un oiseau à la vie très longue qui se régénère ou renaît de manière cyclique, généralement dans un immense brasier ravageant tout autour de lui. Il symbolise depuis toujours la renaissance, le renouveau et la persévérance.<br><br>Les phénix ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les attaques d'un phénix peuvent enflammer ses cibles. À sa mort, il explose et laisse un œuf résistant au feu, parfois deux. Au bout d'un certain temps, un nouveau phénix en éclot. |  |

| `MM_Qilin.label` | qilin | qilin | qilin |  |
| `MM_Qilin.description` | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | Le qilin est une créature mythique et chimérique à sabots connue en Chine et dans d'autres cultures d'Asie orientale. Il annoncerait l'arrivée ou la mort prochaine d'un sage ou d'un souverain illustre. Son corps, semblable à celui d'un cerf, est partiellement couvert d'écailles, et sa tête porte une paire de bois impressionnants.<br><br>Les qilins ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Habituellement paisibles, les qilins ont cependant la fâcheuse tendance à enflammer leurs ennemis lorsqu'on les provoque. |  |

| `MM_Salamander.description` | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | La salamandre est un amphibien auquel, comme à beaucoup d'animaux réels, on a souvent attribué des qualités fantastiques et parfois occultes qu'il ne possède pas. Dans les traditions antiques, médiévales et de la Renaissance, la salamandre légendaire prend souvent l'aspect d'une salamandre ordinaire ou d'un serpent aux traits de lézard, mais on lui prête généralement une affinité avec le feu élémentaire.<br><br>Les salamandres ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Elles ont été créées dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les salamandres dégagent une chaleur incroyable, ce qui les rend très dangereuses, ou très utiles, dans les espaces clos. Leurs œufs partagent cette propriété et explosent à l'éclosion. La morsure d'une salamandre peut enflammer sa cible. |  |

| `MM_StymphalianBird.description` | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | Les oiseaux du lac Stymphale sont des oiseaux voraces de la mythologie grecque, nommés d'après le marais de Stymphale où ils vivaient. Mangeurs d'humains, ils avaient un bec de bronze, des plumes métalliques tranchantes qu'ils projetaient sur leurs victimes et des fientes empoisonnées. Les vaincre constituait le sixième travail d'Héraclès.<br><br>Les oiseaux du lac Stymphale ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les oiseaux du lac Stymphale survolent le terrain et ignorent ses pénalités de déplacement. Ils disposent d'attaques tranchantes à distance. |  |

| `MM_Tlilcoatl.description` | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | Le tlilcoatl, « serpent noir » en nahuatl, est un reptile prédateur du folklore mexicain. Il possède des écailles noires luisantes, une queue bifide et peut atteindre une taille considérable. Il exhale un dangereux gaz toxique qui étouffe rapidement ses victimes.<br><br>Les tlilcoatls ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les tlilcoatls sont extrêmement venimeux, avec une morsure dangereuse et un souffle particulièrement redoutable. Ils n'attaquent toutefois les humains que pour se défendre. |  |

| `MM_Unicorn.description` | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | La licorne est une créature légendaire décrite depuis l'Antiquité comme une bête portant une unique et longue corne pointue en spirale au milieu du front. Elle figurait sur les anciens sceaux de la civilisation de la vallée de l'Indus et était mentionnée par les Grecs anciens. On la décrivait couramment comme une créature des bois extrêmement farouche, symbole de pureté et de grâce. Sa corne aurait le pouvoir de purifier l'eau empoisonnée et de guérir les maladies.<br><br>Les licornes ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Elles ont été créées dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les licornes sont d'excellentes montures et bénéficient d'un important bonus de vitesse lorsqu'elles sont montées. Cette capacité nécessite le mod Giddy-Up 2 - Continued (Hue, coco 2, repris). |  |

| `MM_WildMinotaur.description` | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | Dans la mythologie grecque, le Minotaure était représenté avec la tête et la queue d'un taureau sur un corps humain. Il vivait au centre du Labyrinthe, construction complexe conçue par Dédale et son fils Icare sur l'ordre du roi Minos de Crète. Il fut finalement tué par le héros athénien Thésée. Le Minotaure mythologique était souvent rusé, tandis que cette créature ne possède qu'une intelligence instinctive très rudimentaire.<br><br>Les minotaures ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : les minotaures sauvages peuvent frapper le sol de leurs sabots pour provoquer des secousses. |  |

| `MM_WillOWisp.label` | will-o'-wisp | will-o'-wisp | feu follet |  |
| `MM_WillOWisp.description` | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | Le feu follet est une créature du folklore anglais, une lueur fantomatique aperçue la nuit par les voyageurs, surtout au-dessus des tourbières et des marécages. Il égarerait les passants en ressemblant à la lumière vacillante d'une lampe ou d'une lanterne.<br><br>Les feux follets ne sont pas des créatures naturelles. Ils ont été créés dans le cadre du projet Hécate, un programme de bio-ingénierie utilisant des réplicateurs mécanites pour transformer des animaux existants en créatures « magiques ».<br><br>Capacités spéciales : on peut à peine considérer les feux follets comme des créatures vivantes ; ils ressemblent surtout à des bactéries vraiment étranges. Ils se reproduisent par fission asexuée, ont besoin de très peu de nourriture et sont immunisés contre les températures extrêmes, les environnements toxiques, le feu et les attaques psychiques. Lorsqu'ils sont menacés, ce qui est rare, ils peuvent souffler un nuage de particules multicolores apparemment nocives pour les êtres vivants. |  |

Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | Le xiezhi est une créature légendaire de la mythologie chinoise, également connue en Corée sous le nom de haetae. | `MM_ToxicBite.description` | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | 血流中の毒素.これは,環境毒素,有毒な咬傷,毒された武器など,さまざまな原因から発生する可能性があります.<br><br>高用量では,毒性の蓄積が致命的です.低用量でさえ,それは癌を生成する可能性があります.<br><br>ベースのゲームの有毒な蓄積とは対照的に,この有毒な損傷で死亡した生物は食べることができます. |  |

| `MM_Ahuizotl.description` | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. | `MM_Basilisk.description` | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | ヨーロッパの動物寓話には,バジリスクは伝説的な爬虫類であり,蛇の王様と呼ばれており,一瞥しただけで死を招くことができると言われています.長老プリニウスは,バジリスクを"頭に白い斑点があり,王冠のような形をした蛇"と表現しています.バジリスクは植物やあらゆる種類の植生に非常に有害な影響を与え,周囲の植物を破壊して腐らせると言われていました.<br><br>バジリスクは特に不快な獣です.その視線は対象を石化させることができ,周囲の草や茂みはゆっくりと腐って枯れた殻になり,その過程でバジリスクのエサになります.幸いなことに,バジリスクはあまり姿を見せることはありません. |  |

| `MM_Cerberus.description` | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | ギリシャ神話では,ケルベロスはしばしばハーデスの猟犬と呼ばれ,死者が出て行かないように冥界の門を守る3つの頭を持つ犬です.ケルベロスは,エキドナとテュポーンの子孫であり,ヘラクレスの12の功業の1つであるヘラクレスによって捕獲されたと知られています.ケルベロスは,普通の犬よりも訓練するのが難しく知能も高くないですが,3つの頭を強化顎で改造した場合は非常に危険な戦闘動物になります. |  |

| `MM_ErymanthianBoar.description` | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | エリュマントスの猪は,エリュマントス山の斜面に生息する巨大な猪であり,プソーピス一帯の田畑や農村を荒らして回っていました. |  |

| `MM_Fenghuang.label` | fenghuang | fenghuang | 鳳凰 |  |
| `MM_Fenghuang.description` | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | 鳳凰は東アジアの神話に登場する伝説的な鳥で,高徳と優雅さを象徴しています.鳳凰はまた,陰と陽の結びつきを象徴しており,この鳥は最高の平和と繁栄,または幸福に恵まれた地域や場所にのみ現れると信じられています.伝統的には,多くの鳥の複合体として表現され,しばしば「中国のフェニックス」とも呼ばれていますが,西洋のフェニックスとの類似点は表面的なものです.<br><br>飼いならされた鳳凰は平和と静寂のオーラを放ち,近くの人々の精神を高揚させます. |  |

| `MM_Griffin.description` | The griffin is a creature with the body, tail, and back legs of a lion; the head and wings of an eagle; and an eagle's talons as its front feet. Griffins are especially powerful and majestic creatures, known for guarding treasure and priceless possessions, and valued as trusted steeds.<br><br>Griffins aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Griffins are excellent mounts, receiving bonuses to speed and armour when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Griffins are especially powerful and majestic creatures, known for guarding treasure and priceless possessions, and valued as trusted steeds.<br><br>Griffins aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Griffins are excellent mounts, receiving bonuses to speed and armour when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! | `MM_Ieltxu.description` | The ieltxu is a genie bird in Basque mythology. Usually depicted as a fire-breathing, pitch-black raven, the ieltxu is not a malicious creature, though it is often described as mischievous. Ieltxu are creatures of the night, usually appearing in dark forests under the cover of darkness.<br><br>Ieltxu aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Ieltxu can breathe fire when threatened. They are nocturnal creatures that sleep during the day. | The ieltxu is a genie bird in Basque mythology. Usually depicted as a fire-breathing, pitch-black raven, the ieltxu is not a malicious creature, though it is often described as mischievous. Ieltxu are creatures of the night, usually appearing in dark forests under the cover of darkness.<br><br>Ieltxu aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Ieltxu can breathe fire when threatened. They are nocturnal creatures that sleep during the day. | イエルチュウはバスク神話に登場する精霊の鳥です.通常は火を吐く真っ黒なカラスとして描かれていますが,悪意のある生き物ではありませんが,いたずら好きとよく言われます.イエルチュウは夜の生き物であり,通常,暗闇に覆われた暗い森の中に現れます.<br><br>イエルチュウは脅威に晒されると火を吐きます.昼間に眠る夜行性の生き物です. |  |

| `MM_Kappa.label` | kappa | kappa | 河童 |  |
| `MM_Kappa.description` | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | 河童は,日本の民間伝承に登場する川の小鬼で,水かきのある手足と亀のような甲羅を背負った緑色の人間のような姿で描かれています.頭にくぼみがあり,「皿」が少量の水を溜まっており,この水がこぼれたり乾いたりすると非常に弱ってしまいます.河童はいたずら好きと思われがちですが,本質的に悪意のあるものではありません.実際,友達になれば,河童は農家の人たちの土地の灌漑を手伝うなど,人間のためにさまざまな仕事をしてくれるようになります.<br><br>河童は臆病で,HPを失いすぎると戦闘から逃げ出します.適切に訓練された河童(少なくとも"リリース"スキル)は,収穫作業において入植者を助けることができます. |  |

| `MM_Kitsune.description` | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | 妖狐は日本の民間伝承の伝説的な生き物であり,年齢を経るほどに知恵の増大する,超常的な能力を備えた非常に知的な生物です.尾の数が多いほど古くから生きており強大な妖狐です. |  |

| `MM_LernaeanHydra.description` | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | ヒュドラはギリシャとローマの神話に登場する,蛇紋岩の怪物です.アルゴリス地方のレルネー湖を隠れ家にしています.ヒュドラは非常に有毒な息と血液,および強力な再生能力を備えています. |  |

| `MM_LesserWyvern.label` | lesser wyvern | lesser wyvern | レッサーワイバーン |  |
| `MM_LesserWyvern.description` | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | 中世ヨーロッパの紋章学では,ワイバーンは翼のある二足歩行のドラゴンであり,多くの紋章旗に登場します.紋章学や民間伝承でのワイバーンは,4本足のドラゴンとは異なり,火を吐くことはほとんどありません.その代わりに,ワイバーンは毒牙や危険なとげのある尾に毒を持っています.<br><br>レッサーワイバーンは非常に強い毒を持っており,ダメージに強く,極端な痛みにはほとんど気にしません.幸いなことに,彼らは非常に珍しいです.ワイバーンとは異なり,彼らは毒を吐くことができません（ワイバーンを見るために,RimWorld-Witcher_Monster_Hunt_modをダウンロードしてください！） |  |

| `MM_Manticore.label` | manticore | manticore | マンティコア |  |
| `MM_Manticore.description` | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. | `MM_Pegasus.description` | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up!
