| `MM_Phoenix.description` | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | 古代エジプトやギリシャ,ローマ神話では,フェニックスは,周期的に再生するか,またはそれ以外の方法で生まれ変わる長寿の鳥であり,しばしば大規模な大火で周囲のあらゆるものを焼き尽くしてしまいます.象徴的に,フェニックスは常に再生,再生と忍耐の概念に関連付けられています.<br><br>フェニックスの攻撃は,発火する可能性があります.フェニックスが死ぬと爆発し,難燃性の卵（2つの場合もある）を生み出します.しばらくすると,この卵から新しいフェニックスが孵化します. |  |

| `MM_Qilin.description` | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. | `MM_Salamander.label` | salamander | salamander | サラマンダー |  |
| `MM_Salamander.description` | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | サラマンダーは両生類であり,多くの実在の生物と同様に,実在の生物が持っていない幻想的な,時には神秘的な性質を持っているとされてきました.古典的,中世,ルネッサンスの伝説のサラマンダーは,しばしば典型的なサンショウウオやトカゲのような形をした蛇として描かれていますが,通常は元素の火との親和性を持っているとされています.<br><br>サラマンダーは信じられないほどの熱量を放射するので,狭い場所では非常に危険な（あるいは非常に有用な）存在になります.サラマンダーの卵にもその性質があり,孵化すると爆発します.サラマンダーに咬まれると発火します. |  |
| `MM_Salamander.tools.firefangs.label` | fire-fangs | fire-fangs | 炎の牙 |  |
| `MM_Salamander.tools.head.label` | head | head | 頭部 |  |
| `MM_StymphalianBird.label` | stymphalian bird | stymphalian bird | ステュムパーロスの鳥 |  |
| `MM_StymphalianBird.description` | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | ステュムパーロスの鳥は,ギリシャ神話に登場する貪欲な鳥類で,その名はステュムパーロス湖畔に棲息していたことに由来しています.青銅製の嘴,鋭い金属の羽,毒のある糞を持つ人喰い鳥でした.ヘラクレスの第6の大業は,この鳥を退治することでした.<br><br>ステュムパーロスの鳥は空を飛び,地形の移動コストを無視する特殊能力を持っています.鋭い羽を飛ばして遠隔攻撃をすることもできます. |  |

| `MM_Tlilcoatl.description` | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | トリルコアトル（ナワトル語で「黒い蛇」）は,メキシコの民間伝承における捕食性爬虫類です.トリルコアトルは光沢のある黒い鱗と二股に分かれた尾を持ち,かなりの大きさに成長します.彼らは危険な有毒ガスを吐くことができ,犠牲者をすぐに窒息させます.<br><br>トリルコアトルは強力な毒を持ち,危険な噛みつきと非常に厄介なブレスの攻撃をします.しかし,彼らは防衛反応として襲撃者に対して攻撃するだけです. |  |

| `MM_Unicorn.label` | unicorn | unicorn | ユニコーン |  |
| `MM_Unicorn.description` | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | ユニコーンは,額から突き出た一本の大きく尖った螺旋状の角を持つ獣として描かれてきた伝説の生き物です.ユニコーンの角には毒を飲めるようにし,病気を治す力があると言われており,とても獰猛な生物だが処女の胸に抱かれると大人しくなるという伝承があります. |  |

| `MM_WildMinotaur.description` | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | 雄牛の頭と尾,そして人間の体を持つギリシャ神話の怪物です.クレタ島のミノス王の指示で,建築家のダイダロスと息子のイカロスによって設計された精巧な迷宮を住処にしていました. |  |

| `MM_WillOWisp.description` | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | ウィル・オー・ウィスプは,英国の民間伝承の生き物です.ランプやランタンの光と思わせて旅人を惑わせると言われています. |  |

| `MM_Xiezhi.description` | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. The xiezhi is usually represented as a cross between a lion and a goat, with a single horn on its forehead. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | 獬豸(カイチ)は中国神話や韓国神話ではヘテと呼ばれる伝説的な生き物です.獬豸(カイチ)は通常,ライオンとヤギを掛け合わせたような形をしていて,額に1本の角があります.獬豸(カイチ)は生まれつき善悪に通じていて,罪なき人と罪人を本能的に見分けることができます.<br><br>獬豸(カイチ)は裁きの角で敵対者に対して激しく攻撃をします. |  |

Having them in melee might not be the best use for them. | この動物は,遠距離攻撃を使って目標を麻痺させます.この動物に近接攻撃させることは,ベストな使い方とは言えません. |  |
| `MM_ParalyzingShockTroopDesc` | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects.<br><br>This animal also has attacks that can be used to immobilize attackers. | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects.<br><br>This animal also has attacks that can be used to immobilize attackers. | 「打撃」役割の動物は高速移動に特化しており,近接戦闘が有利になる能力を持ちます.それらは防御力やHPが高いわけではありませんが,適切に使役すれば敵を壊滅させる事が可能です.<br><br>この動物は,攻撃された相手を麻痺させる攻撃を持っています. |  |
| `MM_PrimaryRole` | Primary Role | Primary Role | 優先役割 |  |
| `MM_Ranged` | This animal uses ranged attacks, and will only use melee attacks if the enemy gets into melee range. To order it to attack, it needs to be trained in Guard and Attack, then it needs to be assigned to follow a master when drafted and/or working. The "release" button on the master's commands bar will make the animal search for appropriate cover and use its ranged attack. | This animal uses ranged attacks, and will only use melee attacks if the enemy gets into melee range. To order it to attack, it needs to be trained in Guard and Attack, then it needs to be assigned to follow a master when drafted and/or working. The "release" button on the master's commands bar will make the animal search for appropriate cover and use its ranged attack. | この動物は遠隔攻撃を行い,敵から近接攻撃を受けた場合だけ近接攻撃を行います.攻撃を命令するには,防御と攻撃が訓練済で,その後,徴兵または屋外活動時に追従を割り当てられている必要があります.主人のコマンドバーにある「獲物を指示」ボタンを押すと,動物は適当な目標を探して遠距離攻撃を仕掛けます. |  |
| `MM_RangedAttacks` | Uses Ranged Attacks | Uses Ranged Attacks | 遠距離攻撃を使用する |  |
| `MM_SecondaryRole` | Secondary Role | Secondary Role | 二次役割 |  |
| `MM_ShockTroop` | Shock Troop | Shock Troop | 打撃 |  |
| `MM_ShockTroopDOTDesc` | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects.<br><br>This animal has a melee attack that causes damage over time. | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects.<br><br>This animal has a melee attack that causes damage over time. | 「打撃」役割の動物は高速移動に特化しており,近接戦闘が有利になる能力を持ちます.それらは防御力やHPが高いわけではありませんが,適切に使役すれば敵を壊滅させる事が可能です.<br><br>この動物は,時間の経過とともにダメージを与えるDOT攻撃を持っています. |  |
| `MM_ShockTroopDesc` | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects. | "Shock Troop" animals combine high speed with an ability that adds utility to melee combat. They may not be very resistant, or have high health, but if properly used they can have devastating effects. | 「打撃」役割の動物は高速移動に特化しており,近接戦闘が有利になる能力を持ちます.それらは防御力やHPが高いわけではありませんが,適切に使役すれば敵を壊滅させる事が可能です.<br><br>この動物は,時間の経過とともにダメージを与えるDOT攻撃を持っています. |  |
| `MM_Tank` | Tank | Tank | 前衛 |  |
| `MM_Utility` | Utility | Utility | 補助動物 |  |
| `MM_UtilityHealthDesc` | This animal slowly heals nearby colonists and animals. | This animal slowly heals nearby colonists and animals. | この動物は体が非常に弱く,戦闘には向いていません.ただし,傷を治療したり,何らかの方法で強化したして,入植者の健康維持を補助します. |  |
| `MM_UtilityMoodDesc` | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however increase the mood of your colonists, either through nuzzling or other abilities. | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however increase the mood of your colonists, either through nuzzling or other abilities. | この動物は体が非常に弱く,戦闘には向いていません.ただし,すり寄りや他の代替能力を通して,入植者の心情を高めます. |  |
| `MM_UtilityTemperatureDesc` | This animal radiates heat, warming its surroundings. | This animal radiates heat, warming its surroundings. | この動物は体が非常に弱く,戦闘には向いていません.ただし,コロニーの熱量制御に役立ちます. |  |
| `MM_UtilityWorkerDesc` | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however help your colonists with certain tasks (Mining, Harvesting, etc.), considerably reducing their workload. | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however help your colonists with certain tasks (Mining, Harvesting, etc.), considerably reducing their workload. | この動物は体が非常に弱く,戦闘には向いていません.ただし,入植者が特定の作業 (採掘,収穫,清掃など) を行うのに役立ち,作業負荷が大幅に軽減されます. |  |
| `MM_VenomousTankDesc` | "Tank" animals are specialized in melee combat. These animals have high attack damage, good resistance, high health, or other abilities that allow them to survive in the front lines.<br><br>This animal also has powerful venomous attacks. | "Tank" animals are specialized in melee combat. These animals have high attack damage, good resistance, high health, or other abilities that allow them to survive in the front lines.<br><br>This animal also has powerful venomous attacks. | 「打撃」役割の動物は高速移動に特化しており,近接戦闘が有利になる能力を持ちます.それらは防御力やHPが高いわけではありませんが,適切に使役すれば敵を壊滅させる事が可能です.<br><br>この動物は,有毒物質の蓄積を引き起こす毒攻撃を持っています. |  |
| `MM_Yes` | Yes | Yes | はい |  |
| `AMR_SettingsCategory` | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) |  |
| `AMR_SpawnMultiplier` | Wild spawn frequency: ×{0} | Wild spawn frequency: ×{0} | 野生での出現頻度: ×{0} |  |
| `AMR_SpawnMultiplierTip` | Multiplies how common every creature of this mod is among the wild animals of a biome (1 = the mod's own values). It changes their share compared with other animals, not the total number of animals on the map. Applies immediately, to new spawns only: animals already on the map stay. | Multiplies how common every creature of this mod is among the wild animals of a biome (1 = the mod's own values). It changes their share compared with other animals, not the total number of animals on the map. Applies immediately, to new spawns only: animals already on the map stay. | このMODの全ての生き物が、バイオーム内の野生動物の中でどれだけ多く現れるかを倍率で調整します(1 = MOD本来の値)。他の動物との割合が変わるだけで、マップ上の動物の総数は変わりません。すぐに反映されますが、新たな出現にのみ適用され、すでにマップ上にいる動物はそのままです。 |  |
| `AMR_ResetDefaults` | Restore defaults | Restore defaults | 初期値に戻す |  |
| `AMR_AllowedHeader` | Creatures allowed to appear in the wild (unchecked: never spawn; tamed and existing animals are unaffected) | Creatures allowed to appear in the wild (unchecked: never spawn; tamed and existing animals are unaffected) | 野生に出現できる生き物(チェックなし: 出現しません。飼いならされた動物と既存の動物には影響しません) |  |

Normalmente representado como um homem de fogo, preto como um corvo de resina, Ielthu não é um ser malicioso, embora frequentemente seja descrito como opressivo. As criaturas noturnas que geralmente aparecem em florestas escuras sob o escuro .<br><br>Ielthu não são criaturas que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas no projeto Hecate, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mechanite a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>. São seres noturnos que dormem durante o dia. |  |

| `MM_Kappa.label` | kappa | kappa | cappa |  |
| `MM_Kappa.description` | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | Cappa é um traço fluvial no folclore japonês, representado como seres verdes e humanos com braços e pernas trocados e carapuça de tartaruga nas costas. O aprofundamento na cabeça deles, o seu «prato», retém uma pequena quantidade de água, e a cappa enfraquece muito se esta água derramar ou secar. Embora as capas sejam muitas vezes consideradas opressivas, elas não são essencialmente maliciosas. Na verdade, uma vez amigos, a cappa pode realizar quaisquer tarefas para as pessoas, como ajudar os agricultores a irrigar suas terras .<br><br>Kapa não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. As capas são cobardes e fogem do combate se perdem muita saúde. Uma cappa devidamente treinada (pelo menos a habilidade de «Guardião») pode ajudar os colonos a realizar as tarefas de colheita. |  |

Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | Kitsune é uma criatura lendária do folclore japonês, representada como uma criatura bastante inteligente, semelhante à raposa, com poderes paranormais que aumentam com a idade e a sabedoria. | `MM_LernaeanHydra.label` | lernaean hydra | lernaean hydra | Hidra de Lernay |  |
| `MM_LernaeanHydra.description` | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | A Hidra de Lernay é um monstro de água de cobras na mitologia grega e romana. A sua guarita era o Lago Lerna, em Argolide, daí o seu nome. Hidra tem respiração e sangue extremamente venenosos e poderes regenerativos impressionantes. O assassinato da hidra foi o objetivo da Segunda Proeza de Herculano .<br><br>Hidras não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte de um projeto de Hécat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: hidras podem regenerar feridas rapidamente. As hidras são perigosas em combate próximo, mas o verdadeiro perigo vem da respiração ácida venenosa. Felizmente, eles geralmente são seres muito tímidos e não tendem a se aproximar dos humanos. |  |

| `MM_LesserWyvern.label` | lesser wyvern | lesser wyvern | pequena viverna |  |
| `MM_LesserWyvern.description` | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | Na geraldina medieval europeia, a viverna é um dragão de duas pernas alado que aparece em vários estandartes e brasões. Viverna em heráldicas e folclore raramente é uma lançadora de fogo, ao contrário de um dragão de quatro patas. Em vez disso, os viveres são normalmente associados ao veneno, seja como presas venenosas, seja como uma cauda perigosa .<br><br>Os viveres menores não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hécat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. Os pequenos viveiros são extremamente venenosos, resistentes aos danos e quase indiferentes à dor severa. Felizmente, são muito raros. Ao contrário do Big Vivern, eles não podem cuspir veneno (para ver as grandes vivernas, baixem a nossa moda RimWorld - Caçar o Bruxo dos Monstros!). |  |

| `MM_Manticore.label` | manticore | manticore | mantiqueira |  |
| `MM_Manticore.description` | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | Mantikora é uma criatura lendária tremenda na mitologia persa. Ele tem uma cabeça de leão-humanoide híbrida, um corpo de leão e uma cauda de escorpiónio com espinhos venenosos, como agulhas de uma espécie selvagem. Este mantiqueiro também foi modificado para ter asas não unificadas ordenadas, embora as descrições históricas da criatura não incluíam. Mantiquora é um predador feroz capaz de localizar a sua vítima e atacar à queima-roupa .<br><br>Os mantikores não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de tecidos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: manticores podem lançar agulhas envenenadas para fora. |  |

| `MM_Pegasus.description` | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | Pegasus é um mítico cavalo divino alado e uma das criaturas mais reconhecidas da mitologia grega. Pegasus são garanhões brancos com um par de asas operadas a crescer das suas costas. Disseram que os pegas mitológicos podiam voar com estas asas, mas estes seres parecem tê-las apenas por razões estéticas, porque não há tanta engenharia genética que possa criar asas tão frágeis para suportar o seu peso. Eles são apreciados como cavalos rápidos e confiáveis .<br><br>Pegasus não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Gekat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em criaturas «mágicas» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Pegasus são ótimos animais de condução que recebem um bónenorme de velocidade à cavalagem. Maude nos cavalos de combate). |  |

| `MM_Phoenix.description` | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | Na antiga mitologia egípcia, e mais tarde grega e romana, a fênix é um pássaro de longa vida que se regenera cíclicamente ou, de outra forma, nasce de novo, normalmente como resultado de um incêndio de grandes proporções que esvazia tudo. Simbolicamente, a Fênix sempre foi associada a conceitos de renascimento, renovação e tenacidade .<br><br>As fênix não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: os ataques de phopodem podem. Quando Phoenix morrer, vai explodir e criar um ovo de fogo (e, às vezes, dois). Daqui a pouco, um novo Phoenix vai emergir deste ovo. |  |

| `MM_Qilin.description` | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | O Cilino é uma criatura quimérica mítica conhecida nas culturas chinesas e outras da Ásia Oriental, que se diz que aparece com a chegada inevitável ou a morte de um sábio ou de um governante glorificado. Os corpos dos cilinos são parcialmente cobertos de escamas e têm a forma de veado, e as cabeças deles têm um par de chifres impressionantes .<br><br>O Cilin não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seus animais. As criaturas «mágicas »<br><br>Embora o Cilin seja normalmente um ser pacífico, eles têm uma tendência desagradável a incendiar os seus inimigos quando são provocados. |  |

The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | Salandra é um anfíbio que, como muitos seres reais, muitas vezes é atribuído a qualidades fantásticas e algumas vezes ocultas que o organismo real não possui. A lendária salmandra nos relatos clássicos, medievais e renascentistas é frequentemente retratada como uma típica salmandra ou serpente em forma de lagarto, mas é geralmente atribuída a uma afinidade com o elemento do fogo .<br><br>Os salmandras não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos aos animais já existentes icos para mutá-los ". As salmandras emitem quantidades incríveis de calor, tornando-as muito perigosas (ou muito úteis) em um espaço estreito. Os ovos deles também têm essa qualidade, e explodem quando eclodem. | `MM_Tlilcoatl.description` | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | Tililcoatl é um réptil predador no folclore mexicano. Os Tililcoatli têm uma escova preta brilhante, uma cauda dividida e crescem para um tamanho considerável. Eles podem respirar o perigoso gás venenoso que rapidamente sufoca suas vítimas .<br><br>Os Tlilcoatli não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Gekat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes, para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Tlilcoatli extremamente extremamente extremamente. No entanto, eles só atacarão as pessoas se defenderem. |  |

| `MM_Unicorn.description` | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | O unicórnio é uma criatura lendária que, desde a antiguidade, foi descrita como uma besta com um corno grande, afiado, enrolado, em espiral, a sair da sua testa. O unicórnio foi retratado em impressos antigos da civilização do Vale de Inda e foi mencionado por gregos antigos. O unicórnio era geralmente descrito como um ser florestal extremamente selvagem, um símbolo de pureza e elegância. Dizem que o seu córrego tem a capacidade de fazer água envenenada para beber e curar doenças .<br><br>Os unicórnios não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Unicórnios são ótimos animais de condução que recebem um bónus enorme para a velocidade a cavalo. Maude nos cavalos de combate). |  |

| `MM_WildMinotaur.description` | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | Na mitologia grega, Minotauro é uma criatura mítica representada em tempos clássicos com a cabeça e a cauda de um touro e o corpo de um homem. Ele vivia no centro do Labirinto, que era uma estrutura complexa, semelhante a um labirinto projetado pelo arquiteto Dedal e seu filho, Icarus, por ordem do rei crítico Minos. No fim, Minotauro foi morto pelo herói de Atenas Teseu. O Minotauro mitológico foi frequentemente retratado como uma besta astuta, enquanto esta criatura tem uma inteligência muito simples e instintiva .<br><br>Os minotauros não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicava os produtos mecânicos aos animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. |  |

| `MM_WillOWisp.description` | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | A luz vagabunda é uma criatura do folclore inglês composta por uma luz fantasma atmosférica que os viajantes veem à noite, especialmente sobre os pântanos. Dizem que ele ilude os viajantes, lembrando-se de uma lâmpada ou de uma lanterna .<br><br>As luzes vagas não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mecânicos aos animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: luzes brilantes Eles se multiplicam por uma divisão sem controle, quase não precisam de alimentos e são imunes a mudanças de temperatura, ambientes tóxicos, fogo e ataques mentais. Se forem ameaçados, o que não acontece com frequência, podem expelir uma nuvem de partículas coloridas que parece ser prejudicial para os seres vivos. |  |

| `MM_Xiezhi.description` | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. The xiezhi is usually represented as a cross between a lion and a goat, with a single horn on its forehead. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. The xiezhi is usually represented as a cross between a lion and a goat, with a single horn on its forehead. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | Sechi é uma criatura lendária na mitologia chinesa e na mitologia coreana, onde é conhecida como Hataé. As secas são normalmente retratadas como algo entre um leão e um bode, com o único chifre na testa. As secções são inidôneas para o bem e o mal, sendo capazes de distinguir instintivamente os inocentes dos culpados .<br><br>Seji não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas como parte do Projeto Gekat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de tecidos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Seque serão ferozes. |  |

No entanto, isso ajudará a saúde dos vossos colonos, seja curando as feridas ou de alguma forma fortalecendo-as. |  |
| `MM_UtilityMoodDesc` | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however increase the mood of your colonists, either through nuzzling or other abilities. | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however increase the mood of your colonists, either through nuzzling or other abilities. | Este animal não é adaptado para lutar, sendo muito fraco fisicamente. No entanto, isso aumentará o humor dos vossos colonos, seja através do cheiramento ou de outras capacidades alternativas. |  |
| `MM_UtilityTemperatureDesc` | This animal radiates heat, warming its surroundings. | This animal radiates heat, warming its surroundings. | Este animal não é adaptado para lutar, sendo muito fraco fisicamente. No entanto, isso vai ajudá-lo a controlar a temperatura da colónia. |  |
| `MM_UtilityWorkerDesc` | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however help your colonists with certain tasks (Mining, Harvesting, etc.), considerably reducing their workload. | This animal is not suited for combat, being quite physically weak. It will however help your colonists with certain tasks (Mining, Harvesting, etc.), considerably reducing their workload. | Este animal não é adaptado para lutar, sendo muito fraco fisicamente. No entanto, isso ajudará seus colonos com tarefas específicas (mineração, colheita etc), reduzindo significativamente sua carga de trabalho.. |  |
| `MM_VenomousTankDesc` | "Tank" animals are specialized in melee combat. These animals have high attack damage, good resistance, high health, or other abilities that allow them to survive in the front lines.<br><br>This animal also has powerful venomous attacks. | "Tank" animals are specialized in melee combat. These animals have high attack damage, good resistance, high health, or other abilities that allow them to survive in the front lines.<br><br>This animal also has powerful venomous attacks. | Os animais Tanque são especializados em combate próximo. Estes animais têm alto impacto de ataque, boa resistência, alto nível de saúde ou outros poderes que lhes permitem sobreviver na linha de frente .<br><br>Este animal também possui poderosos ataques venenosos. |  |
| `MM_Yes` | Yes | Yes | Sim, sim |  |
| `AMR_SettingsCategory` | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) | Alpha Mythology Renew (unofficial) |  |
| `AMR_SpawnMultiplier` | Wild spawn frequency: ×{0} | Wild spawn frequency: ×{0} | Frequência de aparição em estado selvagem: ×{0} |  |
| `AMR_SpawnMultiplierTip` | Multiplies how common every creature of this mod is among the wild animals of a biome (1 = the mod's own values). It changes their share compared with other animals, not the total number of animals on the map. Applies immediately, to new spawns only: animals already on the map stay. | Multiplies how common every creature of this mod is among the wild animals of a biome (1 = the mod's own values). It changes their share compared with other animals, not the total number of animals on the map. Applies immediately, to new spawns only: animals already on the map stay. | Multiplica a frequência de todas as criaturas deste mod entre os animais selvagens de um bioma (1 = valores do mod). Muda a sua proporção em relação aos outros animais, não o número total de animais no mapa. Aplica-se imediatamente, apenas a novas aparições: os animais já presentes no mapa mantêm-se. |  |
| `AMR_ResetDefaults` | Restore defaults | Restore defaults | Repor valores predefinidos |  |
| `AMR_AllowedHeader` | Creatures allowed to appear in the wild (unchecked: never spawn; tamed and existing animals are unaffected) | Creatures allowed to appear in the wild (unchecked: never spawn; tamed and existing animals are unaffected) | Criaturas autorizadas a aparecer em estado selvagem (desmarcadas: nunca aparecem; os animais domesticados e já existentes não são afetados) |  |

Crepuscular animals are mainly active during dusk. | Determines when the animal sleeps and when it is awake.<br><br>Diurnal animals sleep during the night and are awake during the day, while nocturnal animals sleep during the day and are awake during the night. Crepuscular animals are mainly active during dusk. | Determina quando o animal dorme e quando está acordado. Os animais do dia dormem à noite e acordam durante o dia, enquanto os animais da noite dormem durante o dia e acordam à noite. Os animais crepúsculos são essencialmente ativos no anoitecer. |  |

## PortugueseBrazilian/DefInjected/HediffDef/AlphaMythology.xml

| Key or path | Original | English | PortugueseBrazilian | ? |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `MM_ToxicBite.description` | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxinas na corrente sanguínea. Isto pode vir de várias fontes, incluindo toxinas ambientais, picadas venenosas ou armas envenenadas.<br>Com doses elevadas, a acumulação de toxinas causa a morte. Mesmo em doses baixas, pode causar cancro.<br>\ n Ao contrário da acumulação de toxinas no jogo básico, as criaturas que morrem com este dano tóxico podem ser comidas. |  |

| `MM_Ahuizotl.label` | ahuizotl | ahuizotl | achuizotl |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.description` | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | Ahuizotl é uma criatura lendária de mitos aztecas. Ele é descrito como um cão, a sua pele impenetrável é muitas vezes derramada, formando espinhos. O ahuizotl tem mãos manipuláveis e uma mão extra na cauda. Os ahuizotles são temidos pelo seu vício na carne humana, especialmente nas unhas, olhos e dentes. Dizem que ele vive na água ou perto dela e usa a mão na ponta de sua cauda para agarrar suas presas, arrastando o homem para afogá-lo .<br><br>Os Ahuizotly não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de tecidos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres mágicos. Os ahuizotli usam a mão na ponta da cauda para agarrar suas presas e agarrar-se a elas com as patas, tornando a retirada bastante difícil se lutarem em combate próximo. |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.tools.grabbing_hand.label` | grabbing hand | grabbing hand | Mão agarradora |  |

| `MM_Ahuizotl.tools.right_claw.label` | right claw | right claw | garra direita |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.tools.head.label` | head | head | cabeça |  |
| `MM_Basilisk.label` | basilisk | basilisk | vasilisk |  |
| `MM_Basilisk.description` | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | Nos bestiários europeus, o vasílico é um réptil lendário, considerado o rei da serpente, que pode causar a morte com um único olhar. O Plínio Senior descreveu a vasílica como uma cobra com uma mancha branca na cabeça que parece muito diadema. Eles disseram que ele tem efeitos extremamente nocivos sobre as plantas e todos os tipos de vegetação, destruindo e encurralando todas as plantas que o rodeavam .<br><br>Vassiliski não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres mágicos.\ O seu ataque com o olhar pode petrificar as suas vítimas, e a erva e os arbustos que o rodeiam vão apodrecer lentamente e tornar-se numa seda decadente, alimentando o vasilisco durante o processo. Felizmente, as vasilhas não são muito frequentes. |  |

It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | The catoblepas is a legendary creature from Ethiopia, said to have the body of a cape buffalo and an oversized head that is constantly pointing downwards due to its great weight.<br><br>Catoblepas aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The stare of the catoblepas can turn people into stone. It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | Catoblepas é uma criatura lendária da Etiópia, diz-se que tem um corpo de búfalo de Capá e uma cabeça enorme. que é constantemente apontada para baixo devido ao seu grande peso .<br><br>Os catobleps não são seres, os que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hécata, um programa de engenharia biológica que aplicava replicadores de mecânicos para animais já existentes. | `MM_Cerberus.description` | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | Na mitologia grega, Cerberus, frequentemente chamado de Cerbero Aida, é um cão de três cabeças que protege os portões do Submundo para evitar que os mortos saiam. Cerberus era descendente dos monstros Yehidna e Tifone e, principalmente, conhecido por ter sido capturado por Hércules, um dos doze feitos de Hércules .<br><br>Cerber não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados no Projeto Heckat, um programa de bioengenharia que replicava os mecânicos aos animais já existentes para mutá-los ". Os cerberos são mais difíceis de traçar do que os cães normais, e não são tão inteligentes, mas ter três cabeças torna-os animais de guerra muito, muito perigosos, especialmente quando os implantes de mandíbula biônicos são modificados. |  |

They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Ceryneian Hinds produce milk. | In Greek mythology, the Ceryneian Hind was a mythical creature that lived in Ceryneia, Greece, and took the form of an enormous deer, larger than a bull, with golden antlers like a stag. They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Devolvê-lo vivo Ewrisfei em Mikens foi a terceira proeza de Hércules .<br><br>Os hindos de Kerinei não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: A lanchonete de Kerinei (e, portanto, Hart, porque foram criados em ambos os sexos) é uma criatura muito rápida, embora não possa ser treinada como animal de condução, nem unicórnios ou pegasus. Chimeras just LOVE wooden colonies. Chimeras just LOVE wooden colonies. | `MM_ErymanthianBoar.description` | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | O javali Erimantus era um porco gigante e terrivel que vivia nas encostas do Monte Erimantus, que de vez em quando devastava os campos dos agricultores. A captura do javali era o objetivo do quarto feito de Hércules. Para capturar o javali, Herculano encurralou-o em uma espessa neve, abrandando consideravelmente o seu avanço e vantagem .<br><br>Os javalis erimantins não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de tecidos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos».\ Presas de javali erimantino podem causar vários ferimentos graves que continuam a sangrar e causam mais ferimentos, tornando-os MUITO perigosos em combate próximo. |  |

| `MM_Fenghuang.description` | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | The fenghuang is a legendary bird in East Asian mythologies symbolizing high virtue and grace. The fenghuang also symbolizes the union of yin and yang, and it is believed that the bird only appears in areas or places that are blessed with utmost peace and prosperity or happiness. Traditionally described as a composite of many birds, it is also often called a "Chinese phoenix", although similarities with the western phoenix are superficial.<br><br>Fenghuangs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tamed Fenghuangs radiate an aura of peace and tranquility, elevating the spirits of those nearby. | Fanhuan é um pássaro lendário nas mitologias da Ásia Oriental, que simboliza a alta virtude e elegância. Fanhuan também simboliza a união entre yin e yang, e acredita-se que o pássaro só aparece em áreas ou locais abençoados pela paz e prosperidade ou felicidade. Tradicionalmente descrito como um conjunto de muitos pássaros, ele também é frequentemente chamado de «fênix chinesa», embora a semelhança com o oeste de Phoenix seja superficial .<br><br>Fenhuana não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos».\ |  |

| `MM_Kappa.label` | kappa | kappa | cappa |  |
| `MM_Kappa.description` | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | Cappa é um traço fluvial no folclore japonês, representado como seres verdes e humanos com braços e pernas trocados e carapuça de tartaruga nas costas. O aprofundamento na cabeça deles, o seu «prato», retém uma pequena quantidade de água, e a cappa enfraquece muito se esta água derramar ou secar. Embora as capas sejam muitas vezes consideradas opressivas, elas não são essencialmente maliciosas. Na verdade, uma vez amigos, a cappa pode realizar quaisquer tarefas para as pessoas, como ajudar os agricultores a irrigar suas terras .<br><br>Kapa não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. As capas são cobardes e fogem do combate se perdem muita saúde. Uma cappa devidamente treinada (pelo menos a habilidade de «Guardião») pode ajudar os colonos a realizar as tarefas de colheita. |  |
