| `MM_LernaeanHydra.label` | lernaean hydra | lernaean hydra | Hidra de Lernay |  |
| `MM_LernaeanHydra.description` | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | A Hidra de Lernay é um monstro de água de cobras na mitologia grega e romana. A sua guarita era o Lago Lerna, em Argolide, daí o seu nome. Hidra tem respiração e sangue extremamente venenosos e poderes regenerativos impressionantes. O assassinato da hidra foi o objetivo da Segunda Proeza de Herculano .<br><br>Hidras não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte de um projeto de Hécat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: hidras podem regenerar feridas rapidamente. As hidras são perigosas em combate próximo, mas o verdadeiro perigo vem da respiração ácida venenosa. Felizmente, eles geralmente são seres muito tímidos e não tendem a se aproximar dos humanos. |  |

| `MM_LesserWyvern.label` | lesser wyvern | lesser wyvern | pequena viverna |  |
| `MM_LesserWyvern.description` | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | Na geraldina medieval europeia, a viverna é um dragão de duas pernas alado que aparece em vários estandartes e brasões. Viverna em heráldicas e folclore raramente é uma lançadora de fogo, ao contrário de um dragão de quatro patas. Em vez disso, os viveres são normalmente associados ao veneno, seja como presas venenosas, seja como uma cauda perigosa .<br><br>Os viveres menores não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hécat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. Os pequenos viveiros são extremamente venenosos, resistentes aos danos e quase indiferentes à dor severa. Felizmente, são muito raros. Ao contrário do Big Vivern, eles não podem cuspir veneno (para ver as grandes vivernas, baixem a nossa moda RimWorld - Caçar o Bruxo dos Monstros!). |  |

| `MM_Manticore.label` | manticore | manticore | mantiqueira |  |
| `MM_Manticore.description` | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | Mantikora é uma criatura lendária tremenda na mitologia persa. Ele tem uma cabeça de leão-humanoide híbrida, um corpo de leão e uma cauda de escorpiónio com espinhos venenosos, como agulhas de uma espécie selvagem. Este mantiqueiro também foi modificado para ter asas não unificadas ordenadas, embora as descrições históricas da criatura não incluíam. Mantiquora é um predador feroz capaz de localizar a sua vítima e atacar à queima-roupa .<br><br>Os mantikores não são seres naturais e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de tecidos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: manticores podem lançar agulhas envenenadas para fora. |  |

| `MM_Pegasus.description` | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | Pegasus é um mítico cavalo divino alado e uma das criaturas mais reconhecidas da mitologia grega. Pegasus são garanhões brancos com um par de asas operadas a crescer das suas costas. Disseram que os pegas mitológicos podiam voar com estas asas, mas estes seres parecem tê-las apenas por razões estéticas, porque não há tanta engenharia genética que possa criar asas tão frágeis para suportar o seu peso. Eles são apreciados como cavalos rápidos e confiáveis .<br><br>Pegasus não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Gekat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em criaturas «mágicas» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Pegasus são ótimos animais de condução que recebem um bónenorme de velocidade à cavalagem. Maude nos cavalos de combate). |  |

| `MM_Phoenix.description` | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | Na antiga mitologia egípcia, e mais tarde grega e romana, a fênix é um pássaro de longa vida que se regenera cíclicamente ou, de outra forma, nasce de novo, normalmente como resultado de um incêndio de grandes proporções que esvazia tudo. Simbolicamente, a Fênix sempre foi associada a conceitos de renascimento, renovação e tenacidade .<br><br>As fênix não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: os ataques de phopodem podem. Quando Phoenix morrer, vai explodir e criar um ovo de fogo (e, às vezes, dois). Daqui a pouco, um novo Phoenix vai emergir deste ovo. |  |

| `MM_Qilin.label` | qilin | qilin | cilina |  |
| `MM_Qilin.description` | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | O Cilino é uma criatura quimérica mítica conhecida nas culturas chinesas e outras da Ásia Oriental, que se diz que aparece com a chegada inevitável ou a morte de um sábio ou de um governante glorificado. Os corpos dos cilinos são parcialmente cobertos de escamas e têm a forma de veado, e as cabeças deles têm um par de chifres impressionantes .<br><br>O Cilin não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados no Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos em animais já existentes para mutá-los em seus animais. As criaturas «mágicas »<br><br>Embora o Cilin seja normalmente um ser pacífico, eles têm uma tendência desagradável a incendiar os seus inimigos quando são provocados. |  |

| `MM_Salamander.description` | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | Salandra é um anfíbio que, como muitos seres reais, muitas vezes é atribuído a qualidades fantásticas e algumas vezes ocultas que o organismo real não possui. A lendária salmandra nos relatos clássicos, medievais e renascentistas é frequentemente retratada como uma típica salmandra ou serpente em forma de lagarto, mas é geralmente atribuída a uma afinidade com o elemento do fogo .<br><br>Os salmandras não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos aos animais já existentes icos para mutá-los ". As salmandras emitem quantidades incríveis de calor, tornando-as muito perigosas (ou muito úteis) em um espaço estreito. Os ovos deles também têm essa qualidade, e explodem quando eclodem. A mordida da salmandra pode incendiar o alvo. |  |

| `MM_StymphalianBird.description` | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | The stymphalian birds are a group of voracious birds in Greek mythology, whose name is derived from their dwelling in a swamp in Stymphalia. Stymphalian birds were man-eating birds with beaks of bronze, sharp metallic feathers they could launch at their victims, and poisonous dung. Defeating the stymphalian birds was the objective of the Sixth Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Stymphalian birds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Stymphalian birds fly over terrain, ignoring terrain movement costs. They can use sharp ranged attacks. | As aves de estímulo são um grupo de pássaros ardentes na mitologia grega, cujo nome vem de seu habitat no pântano de Estimulpalia. Os pássaros de Estimulfali eram pássaros oblíquos com bicos de bronze, penas de metal picantes que podiam lançar em suas vítimas e excrementos venenosos. Derrotar os pássaros de estímulo foi o objetivo da Sexta Proeza de Herculano .<br><br>As aves de estímulo não são seres naturais e foram criadas através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mecânicos a animais já existentes para os ignorar em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Os pássaros estimulfalianos voam sobre a localidade. Eles podem usar ataques de longo alcance. |  |

| `MM_Tlilcoatl.description` | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | The tlilcoatl ("black snake" in Nahuatl) is a predatory reptile in Mexican folklore. Tlilcoatls have glossy black scales, a bifurcated tail and grow to a considerable size. They can breathe a dangerous poisonous gas that quickly suffocates their victims.<br><br>Tlilcoatls aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Tlilcoatls are virulently venomous, with a dangerous bite and a very nasty breath attack. They will however only attack humans defensively. | Tililcoatl é um réptil predador no folclore mexicano. Os Tililcoatli têm uma escova preta brilhante, uma cauda dividida e crescem para um tamanho considerável. Eles podem respirar o perigoso gás venenoso que rapidamente sufoca suas vítimas .<br><br>Os Tlilcoatli não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Gekat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores de mecânicos a animais já existentes, para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Tlilcoatli extremamente extremamente extremamente. No entanto, eles só atacarão as pessoas se defenderem. |  |

| `MM_Unicorn.description` | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | O unicórnio é uma criatura lendária que, desde a antiguidade, foi descrita como uma besta com um corno grande, afiado, enrolado, em espiral, a sair da sua testa. O unicórnio foi retratado em impressos antigos da civilização do Vale de Inda e foi mencionado por gregos antigos. O unicórnio era geralmente descrito como um ser florestal extremamente selvagem, um símbolo de pureza e elegância. Dizem que o seu córrego tem a capacidade de fazer água envenenada para beber e curar doenças .<br><br>Os unicórnios não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados através do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mecânicos a animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: Unicórnios são ótimos animais de condução que recebem um bónus enorme para a velocidade a cavalo. Maude nos cavalos de combate). |  |

| `MM_WildMinotaur.description` | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | Na mitologia grega, Minotauro é uma criatura mítica representada em tempos clássicos com a cabeça e a cauda de um touro e o corpo de um homem. Ele vivia no centro do Labirinto, que era uma estrutura complexa, semelhante a um labirinto projetado pelo arquiteto Dedal e seu filho, Icarus, por ordem do rei crítico Minos. No fim, Minotauro foi morto pelo herói de Atenas Teseu. O Minotauro mitológico foi frequentemente retratado como uma besta astuta, enquanto esta criatura tem uma inteligência muito simples e instintiva .<br><br>Os minotauros não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criados como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicava os produtos mecânicos aos animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos »<br><br>. |  |

| `MM_WillOWisp.description` | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | A luz vagabunda é uma criatura do folclore inglês composta por uma luz fantasma atmosférica que os viajantes veem à noite, especialmente sobre os pântanos. Dizem que ele ilude os viajantes, lembrando-se de uma lâmpada ou de uma lanterna .<br><br>As luzes vagas não são seres que se encontram na natureza, e foram criadas como parte do Projeto Hecat, um programa de bioengenharia que aplicou replicadores mecânicos aos animais já existentes para mutá-los em seres «mágicos» .<br><br>Habilidades especiais: luzes brilantes Eles se multiplicam por uma divisão sem controle, quase não precisam de alimentos e são imunes a mudanças de temperatura, ambientes tóxicos, fogo e ataques mentais. Se forem ameaçados, o que não acontece com frequência, podem expelir uma nuvem de partículas coloridas que parece ser prejudicial para os seres vivos. |  |

| `MM_Xiezhi.description` | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. The xiezhi is usually represented as a cross between a lion and a goat, with a single horn on its forehead. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | The xiezhi is a legendary creature in Chinese mythology, as well as in Korean mythology, where it is known as haetae. The xiezhi is usually represented as a cross between a lion and a goat, with a single horn on its forehead. Xiezhi are innately attuned to good and evil, being able to instinctively know the innocent from the guilty.<br><br>Xiezhi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Xiezhi will viciously attack pirates, goring them with their justice-seeking horn. | Sechi é uma criatura lendária na mitologia chinesa e na mitologia coreana, onde é conhecida como Hataé. | `MM_ToxicBite.description` | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Токсин в крови. Может попасть в организм через окружающую среду, укусы ядовитых существ или рану от отравленного оружия.<br><br>Даже в небольших дозах вещество обладает канцерогенным действием, а при больших — смертельно.<br><br>В отличие от отравления токсинами из базовой игры, существа, погибшие от подобного отравления токсинами, пригодны в пищу. |  |

| `MM_Ahuizotl.label` | ahuizotl | ahuizotl | ауисотль |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.description` | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | Ауисотль – легендарное существо из мифов Ацтеков. Оно описывается как собакоподобное, с водонепроницаемым мехом, который часто сбивается в комки, образуя при этом шипы. Ауисотль обладает руками, способными к манипуляциям, а также дополнительной рукой на хвосте. Ауисотля боятся из-за его любви к человеческой плоти, особенно к ногтям, глазам и зубам. Говорят, что он живет в воде или рядом с ней и использует руку на конце хвоста, чтобы схватить добычу и утащить на глубину, чтобы утопить.<br><br>Ауисотль не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности:  Ауисотли умеют использовать руку на конце своего хвоста, чтобы хватать свою добычу и вцепляться в неё лапами, что делает отступление в ближнем бою довольно затруднительным. |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.tools.grabbing_hand.label` | grabbing hand | grabbing hand | хватающая рука |  |

Иелчу — ночные существа, обычно появляющиеся в тёмных лесах под покровом ночи. <br><br>Иелчу не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Иелчу может дышать огнем, когда ему угрожают. Это ночные существа, которые спят днем. |  |

| `MM_Kappa.label` | kappa | kappa | каппа |  |
| `MM_Kappa.description` | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | The kappa is a river imp in Japanese folklore, depicted as green, human-like beings with webbed hands and feet and a turtle-like carapace on their backs. A depression on their head, their "dish", holds a tiny amount of water, and the kappa is severely weakened if this water spills or dries. Although kappa are often seen as mischievous, they are not inherently malicious. In fact, once befriended, kappa may perform any number of tasks for human beings, such as helping farmers irrigate their land.<br><br>Kappa aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Kappa are cowardly, fleeing combat if they lose too much health. Kappa trained in Guard can help colonists with harvesting tasks. | Каппа — это разновидность водяных из японского фольклора, изображаемый в виде зеленого человекоподобного существа с перепончатыми руками и ногами и черепашьим панцирем на спине. Углубление на их голове, их “тарелка”, удерживает мизерное количество воды, и каппа сильно ослабевает, если эта вода проливается или высыхает. Хотя каппа часто считают озорными, по своей сути они не злонамеренны. Фактически, подружившись, каппа может выполнять любое количество задач для людей, например, помогать фермерам орошать их землю.<br><br>Каппа не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Каппа трусливы, бегут из боя, если теряют слишком много здоровья. Надлежащим образом обученный каппа (по крайней мере, навык "Преданность") может помочь колонистам в выполнении задач по сбору урожая. |  |

| `MM_Kitsune.description` | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | The kitsune is a legendary creature from Japanese folklore, depicted as a quite intelligent fox-like being possessing paranormal abilities that increase with its age and wisdom. Kitsune have as many as nine tails. Generally, a greater number of tails indicates an older and more powerful kitsune.<br><br>Kitsune aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: When tamed, kitsune slowly regenerate the wounds of nearby colonists and animals. | Кицунэ — легендарное существо из японского фольклора, изображаемое как довольно умное существо, похожее на лису, обладающее паранормальными способностями, усиливающимися с возрастом и мудростью. У кицунэ целых девять хвостов. Как правило, большее количество хвостов указывает на более старшую и более могущественную кицунэ.<br><br>Кицунэ не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Будучи прирученной, кицунэ медленно заживляет раны ближайшим колонистам и животным. |  |

| `MM_LernaeanHydra.label` | lernaean hydra | lernaean hydra | лернейская гидра |  |
| `MM_LernaeanHydra.description` | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | The Lernaean Hydra is a serpentine water monster in Greek and Roman mythology. Its lair was the lake of Lerna in the Argolid, hence its name. The hydra possesses virulently poisonous breath and blood, and impressive regenerative capabilities. Slaying the hydra was the objective of the Second Labour of Heracles.<br><br>Hydras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Hydras can regenerate wounds quite fast. Hydras are dangerous in close combat, but their real danger comes from their poisonous acidic breath. Luckily, they are usually quite shy creatures and don't tend to approach humans. | Лернейская гидра — змеевидное водное чудовище в греческой и римской мифологии. Её логовом было озеро Лерна в Арголиде, отсюда и его название. Гидра обладает весьма ядовитым дыханием и ядовитой же кровью, а также впечатляющими регенеративными способностями. Убийство гидры было целью Второго подвига Геракла.<br><br>Лернейская гидра не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Гидра может регенерировать раны довольно быстро. Гидры опасны в ближнем бою, но их реальная опасность исходит от ядовитого дыхания. К счастью, они обычно довольно пугливые существа и не склонны подходить к людям. |  |

| `MM_LesserWyvern.label` | lesser wyvern | lesser wyvern | малая виверна |  |
| `MM_LesserWyvern.description` | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | In Medieval European heraldry, the wyvern is a winged bipedal dragon, appearing in numerous standards and coats of arms. The wyvern in heraldry and folklore is rarely fire-breathing, unlike the four-legged dragon. Instead, wyverns are usually associated with poison, either through their venomous fangs or their dangerous barbed tails.<br><br>Lesser wyverns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Lesser wyverns are extremely venomous, resistant to damage, and almost indifferent to extreme pain. Luckily, they are very rare. Unlike Greater wyverns, they can't spit poison (Greater wyverns appear in RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt). | В средневековой европейской геральдике виверна — крылатый двуногий дракон, фигурирующий на многочисленных штандартах и гербах. Виверна в геральдике и фольклоре редко бывает огнедышащей, в отличие от четвероногого дракона. Вместо этого виверны обычно ассоциируются с ядом либо в виде ядовитых клыков, либо в виде их опасного колючего хвоста.<br><br>Малая виверна не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Малые виверны чрезвычайно ядовиты, устойчивы к повреждениям и почти безразличны к сильной боли. К счастью, они очень редки. В отличие от больших виверн, они не могут плеваться ядом (чтобы увидеть больших виверн, установите наш мод RimWorld - Witcher Monster Hunt!). |  |

| `MM_Manticore.label` | manticore | manticore | мантикора |  |
| `MM_Manticore.description` | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | The manticore is a monstrous legendary creature in Persian mythology. It has a hybrid leonine-humanoid head, the body of a lion and a scorpion tail of venomous spines similar to porcupine quills. This manticore has also been modified to have non-functional chiropteran wings, even though historical depictions of the creature didn't include them. The manticore is a fierce predator, capable of stalking its prey and attacking from range.<br><br>Manticores aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Manticores can throw poisoned quills from their scorpion-like tails. | Мантикора — легендарное монструозное существо из персидской мифологии. У него гибридная львино-гуманоидная голова, тело льва и хвост скорпиона с ядовитыми шипами, похожими на иглы дикобраза. Эта мантикора также была модифицирована нефункциональными крыльями рукокрылых, хотя исторические изображения этого существа не включали их. Мантикора — свирепый хищник, способный преследовать свою добычу и атаковать с большого расстояния.<br><br>Мантикора не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Мантикоры могут метать ядовитые иглы из своих скорпионоподобных хвостов. |  |

| `MM_Pegasus.description` | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | The Pegasus is a mythical winged divine horse, and one of the most recognized creatures in Greek mythology. Pegasi are white horses with a pair of feathered wings sprouting from their back. Mythological pegasi were said to be able to fly using those wings, however these creatures seem to sport them just for aesthetic reasons, as there is no amount of genetic engineering that could make such flimsy wings support their weight. They are appreciated as fast and dependable steeds.<br><br>Pegasi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Pegasi are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Battle Mounts mod). | Пегас — мифический крылатый божественный конь и одно из самых узнаваемых существ в греческой мифологии. Пегасы — белые жеребцы с парой пернатых крыльев, растущими из спины. Говорят, что мифологические пегасы могли летать с помощью этих крыльев, однако эти существа, кажется, носят их только из эстетических соображений, поскольку не существует никакой генной инженерии, которая могла бы сделать такие хрупкие крылья способными выдержать вес такого существа. Их ценят как быстрых и надежных скакунов.<br><br>Пегас не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Пегасы – отличные ездовые животные, получающие огромный бонус к скорости, если оседланы (для этого нужен мод Giddy-up! Battle Mounts). |  |

| `MM_Phoenix.description` | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | In ancient Egyptian and later Greek and Roman mythology, a phoenix is a long-lived bird that cyclically regenerates or is otherwise born again, usually in a massive conflagration that lays waste to anything else around it. Symbolically, the phoenix has always been associated with concepts of rebirth, renewal and perseverance.<br><br>Phoenixes aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Attacks from a phoenix can set their targets on fire. When the phoenix dies, it will explode and create a fire-resistant egg (or sometimes two). After a while, a new phoenix will hatch from this egg. | В древнеегипетской, а затем в греческой и римской мифологии феникс — это птица-долгожитель, которая циклически регенерирует или иным образом возрождается, обычно в огромном пожаре, который уничтожает все вокруг себя. Символически Феникс всегда ассоциировался с концепциями возрождения, обновления и стойкости.<br><br>Феникс не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Атаки феникса могут поджечь цель. Когда феникс умирает, он взрывается и создает огнестойкое яйцо (а иногда и два). Через некоторое время из этого яйца вылупится новый феникс. |  |

| `MM_Qilin.label` | qilin | qilin | цилинь |  |
| `MM_Qilin.description` | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | The qilin is a mythical hooved chimerical creature known in Chinese and other East Asian cultures, said to appear with the imminent arrival or passing of a sage or illustrious ruler. Qilins' bodies are partially scaled and shaped like a deer, and they sport a pair of impressive antlers on their heads.<br><br>Qilin aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Though qilins are usually peaceful creatures, they have a nasty tendency to set their enemies on fire when provoked. | Цилинь — это мифическое копытное химерическое существо, известное в китайской и других культурах Восточной Азии, которое, как говорят, появляется с неизбежным прибытием или уходом мудреца или прославленного правителя. Тела цилинь частично покрыты чешуей и имеют форму оленя, а на голове у них пара впечатляющих рогов.<br><br>Цилинь не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Хотя цилинь обычно мирные существа, у них есть неприятная склонность поджигать своих врагов, если их спровоцировать. |  |

| `MM_Salamander.description` | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | The salamander is an amphibian which, as with many real creatures, often has been ascribed fantastic and sometimes occult qualities not possessed by the real organism. The legendary salamander in Classical, Medieval, and Renaissance lore is often depicted as a typical salamander or a snake in shape with a lizard-like form, but is usually ascribed an affinity with elemental fire.<br><br>Salamanders aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Salamanders radiate incredible amounts of heat, making them very dangerous (or very useful) in close spaces. Their eggs have that quality too, and they explode upon hatching. A salamander's bite can set their target on fire. | Саламандра — это амфибия, которой, как и многим реальным существам, часто приписывали фантастические, а иногда и оккультные качества, отсутствующие у реального организма. Легендарная саламандра в классических, средневековых и ренессансных преданиях часто изображается как типичная саламандра или змея в форме с ящерицеобразной формой, но обычно ей приписывают родство со стихией огня.<br><br>Саламандра не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Саламандры излучают невероятное количество тепла, что делает их очень опасными (или очень полезными) в тесных пространствах. Их яйца тоже обладают этим качеством и взрываются при вылуплении. Укус саламандры может поджечь цель. |  |

Стимфалийские птицы были птицами-людоедами с бронзовыми клювами, ядовитым помётом и острыми металлическими перьями, которые они могли метать в своих жертв. | `MM_Unicorn.description` | The unicorn is a legendary creature that has been described since antiquity as a beast with a single large, pointed, spiraling horn projecting from its forehead. The unicorn was depicted in ancient seals of the Indus Valley Civilization and was mentioned by the ancient Greeks. The unicorn was commonly described as an extremely wild woodland creature, a symbol of purity and grace. Its horn is said to have the power to render poisoned water potable and to heal sickness.<br><br>Unicorns aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Unicorns are excellent mounts, receiving a huge bonus to speed when mounted (this needs the Giddy-up! Единорог был изображен на древних печатях цивилизации долины Инда и упоминался древними греками. Единорог обычно описывался как чрезвычайно дикое лесное существо, символ чистоты и грации. Говорят, что его рог обладает способностью делать отравленную воду пригодной для питья и излечивать болезни.<br><br>Единорог не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Единороги – отличные ездовые животные, получающие огромный бонус к скорости, если оседланы (для этого нужен мод Giddy-up! Battle Mounts). |  |

| `MM_WildMinotaur.description` | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | In Greek mythology, the Minotaur is a mythical creature portrayed in Classical times with the head and tail of a bull and the body of a man. He dwelt at the center of the Labyrinth, which was an elaborate maze-like construction designed by the architect Daedalus and his son Icarus, on the command of King Minos of Crete. The Minotaur was eventually killed by the Athenian hero Theseus. The mythological Minotaur was often portrayed as a cunning beast, whereas this creature possesses a very basic, instinctual intelligence.<br><br>Minotaurs aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Wild Minotaurs can pound the ground with their hooves, producing ground tremors. | В греческой мифологии Минотавр — мифическое существо, изображавшееся в классические времена с головой и хвостом быка и телом человека. Он жил в центре Лабиринта, который представлял собой сложное сооружение, спроектированное архитектором Дедалом и его сыном Икаром по приказу критского царя Миноса. В конце концов Минотавр был убит афинским героем Тесеем. Мифологический Минотавр часто изображался как хитрый зверь, тогда как это существо обладает очень простым, инстинктивным интеллектом.<br><br>Минотавр не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Дикие минотавры могут колотить копытами по земле, вызывая сотрясение земли. |  |

| `MM_WillOWisp.description` | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | The will-o'-wisp is a creature from English folklore consisting of an atmospheric ghost light seen by travelers at night, especially over bogs, swamps or marshes. It is said to mislead travelers by resembling a flickering lamp or lantern.<br><br>Will-O'-Wisps aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: will-o'-wisps can barely be considered living creatures, resembling really strange bacteria more than anything else. They reproduce by asexual fission, need almost no sustenance, and are immune to temperature extremes, toxic environments, fire and psychic attacks. If threatened, which isn't often, they can breathe a cloud of multicoloured particles that seems to be harmful to living creatures. | Блуждающий огонёк — существо из английского фольклора, состоящее из атмосферного призрачного света, который путешественники видят ночью, особенно над болотами, топями или трясинами. Говорят, что он вводит путешественников в заблуждение, напоминая мерцающую лампу или фонарь.<br><br>Блуждающий огонёк не является существом, естественно встречающимся в природе, они были созданы в рамках проекта Геката, биоинженерной программы, которая применяла механитовые репликаторы к уже существующим животным, чтобы превратить их в “магических” существ.<br><br>Специальные способности: Блуждающие огоньки вряд ли можно считать живыми существами, они скорее напоминают некие странные бактерии. Они размножаются путём бесполого деления, почти не нуждаются в пище и невосприимчивы к экстремальным температурам, токсичной среде, огню и псионическим атакам. В случае опасности, что бывает нечасто, они могут выдыхать облако разноцветных частиц, которые кажутся вредными для живых существ. |  |

Applies immediately, to new spawns only: animals already on the map stay. | Multiplies how common every creature of this mod is among the wild animals of a biome (1 = the mod's own values). This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. Even at low doses, it can generate cancers.<br><br>Unlike toxic buildup in the base game, creatures that die from this toxic damage can still be eaten. | Toxins in the bloodstream. This can come from various sources, including environmental toxins, venomous bites, or poisoned weapons.<br><br>At high doses, toxic buildup is lethal. | `MM_Ahuizotl.label` | ahuizotl | ahuizotl | ахуїзотль |  |
| `MM_Ahuizotl.description` | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | The ahuizotl is a legendary creature in Aztec myth. It is described as dog-like, with waterproof fur that often clumps into spikes. The ahuizotl has hands capable of manipulation and an additional hand on its tail. The ahuizotl is feared due to its liking for human flesh, especially nails, eyes, and teeth. It is said to live in or near the water and to use the hand on the end of its tail to snatch its prey, dragging the person into the depths to drown them.<br><br>Ahuizotl aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Ahuizotl are adept at using the hand at the end of their tail to grab their prey and claw at them with their paws, making a retreat quite difficult if engaged in close quarters. | Ахуізотль - легендарна істота з ацтекських міфів. Його описують як схожого на собаку, його непромокаємий хутро часто злипається, утворюючи шипи. В ахуїзотля є руки, здатні маніпулювати, і додаткова рука на хвості. Ахуізотля бояться через його пристрасть до людської плоті, особливо нігтям, очам і зубам. Кажуть, що він живе у воді або поруч з нею і використовує руку на кінці свого хвоста, щоб схопити свою здобич, потягуючи людину в глибину, щоб втопити його .<br><br>Ахуізотлі не є істотами, що зустрічаються в природі, і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх у «Волезних». Ахуізотлі вміло використовують руку на кінці свого хвоста, щоб схопити свою здобич і вчепитися в неї лапами, що робить відступ досить важким, якщо вони борються в ближньому бою. |  |

| `MM_Basilisk.label` | basilisk | basilisk | василіск |  |
| `MM_Basilisk.description` | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | In European bestiaries, the basilisk is a legendary reptile, reputed to be a serpent king, who can cause death with a single glance. Pliny the Elder described the basilisk as a serpent with a white spot on the head, strongly resembling a diadem. It was said to have an extremely noxious effect on plants and all kinds of vegetation, destroying and rotting all plants surrounding it.<br><br>Basilisks aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The basilisk is a particularly unpleasant beast. Its gaze attack can petrify its victims, and grass and bushes surrounding it will slowly rot and turn into withered husks, feeding the basilisk in the process. Luckily, basilisks aren't very common. | У європейських бестіаріях василіск - легендарна рептилія, що вважається зміїним королем, який може викликати смерть одним поглядом. Пліній Старший описував василіска як змію з білою плямою на голові, що сильно нагадує діадему. Говорили, що він має надзвичайно шкідливий вплив на рослини і всі види рослинності, знищуючи і загниваючи всі оточуючі його рослини .<br><br>Василиски не є істотами, що зустрічаються в природі, і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх в «чарівних» істот .<br><br>Особливі: Василиск - особливо неприємний звір. Його атака поглядом може скам'янити його жертв, а трава і кущі, що оточують його, повільно гнитимуть і перетворюватимуться на збожеволіле лушпиння в процесі. На щастя, василіски зустрічаються не дуже часто. |  |

| `MM_Catoblepas.description` | The catoblepas is a legendary creature from Ethiopia, said to have the body of a cape buffalo and an oversized head that is constantly pointing downwards due to its great weight.<br><br>Catoblepas aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The stare of the catoblepas can turn people into stone. It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | The catoblepas is a legendary creature from Ethiopia, said to have the body of a cape buffalo and an oversized head that is constantly pointing downwards due to its great weight.<br><br>Catoblepas aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The stare of the catoblepas can turn people into stone. It is not naturally aggressive, but this dangerous ability ensures that it has no natural predators. | Катоблепас - легендарна істота з Ефіопії, кажуть, що у нього тіло капського буйвола і величезна голова, яка постійно спрямована вниз через його велику вагу .<br><br>Катоблепи не є істотами, що зустрічаються в природі, і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх у «Вовщених». Погляд Катоблепаса може перетворювати людей на камінь. Вони не агресивні від природи, але ця небезпечна здатність гарантує, що у них немає природних хижаків. |  |

| `MM_Cerberus.description` | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | In Greek mythology, Cerberus, often referred to as the hound of Hades, is a three-headed dog that guards the gates of the Underworld to prevent the dead from leaving. Cerberus was the offspring of the monsters Echidna and Typhon, and is primarily known for his capture by Heracles, one of Heracles' twelve labours.<br><br>Cerberi aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special abilities: Cerberi are harder to train than regular dogs, and they aren't as intelligent, but having three heads makes them very VERY dangerous combat animals, especially when modified with bionic jaw implants. | У грецькій міфології Цербер, який часто називається гончим Аїда, представляє собою триголову собаку, яка охороняє ворота Підземного світу, щоб не дати мертвим піти. Цербер був нащадком монстрів Єхидни і Тіфона і в першу чергу відомий тим, що був захоплений Гераклом, одним з дванадцяти подвигів Геракла ..<br><br>Цербери не є істотами, що зустрічаються в природі, і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх в "чарівних "\особливостей". церберів важче дресирувати, ніж звичайних собак, і вони не такі розумні, але наявність трьох голів робить їх дуже-дуже небезпечними бойовими тваринами, особливо при модифікації біонічними щелепними імплантатами.. |  |

They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Ceryneian Hinds produce milk. | In Greek mythology, the Ceryneian Hind was a mythical creature that lived in Ceryneia, Greece, and took the form of an enormous deer, larger than a bull, with golden antlers like a stag. To bring it back alive to Eurystheus in Mycenae was the third labour of Heracles.<br><br>Ceryneian Hinds aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Both Ceryneian Hinds and Harts are very fast creatures. They cannot be trained as mounts as effectively as unicorns or pegasi. Ceryneian Hinds produce milk. | У грецькій міфології керінейська лань була міфічною істотою, яка жила в Керінеї, Греція, і приймала форму величезного оленя, більше бика, з золотими рогами, як у оленя. Повернути його живим Еврісфею в Мікени було третім подвигом Геракла .<br><br>Керінейські хінди не є природними істотами і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх в «чарівних» істот .<br><br>Особливі здібності: Керінейська лань (і, відповідно, Харт, оскільки вони були створені в обох підлогах) - дуже швидка істота, хоча її не можна навчити як їздової тварини, а також єдинорогів або пегасів. Він виробляє молоко. |  |

| `MM_Chimera.description` | The chimera was a monstrous fire-breathing hybrid creature of Lycia in Asia Minor, composed of the parts of a lion, with the head of a goat protruding from its back, and a tail that ends with a snake's head.<br><br>Chimeras aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: Chimeras are both slightly venomous and fire-breathing, making them quite dangerous, and not only to their enemies! Chimeras just LOVE wooden colonies. Химери одночасно злегка отруйні і вогнедишать, що робить їх досить небезпечними, і не тільки для їхніх ворогів! Химери просто ОБОЖНЮЮТЬ дерев'яні колонії. О, їх теж можна доїти... |  |

| `MM_ErymanthianBoar.description` | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | The Erymanthian Boar was a gargantuan, monstrous wild hog that inhabited the slopes of Mount Erymanthus, occasionally laying waste to the farmers' fields. The boar's capture was the objective of the Fourth Labour of Heracles. To capture the boar, Heracles drove it into thick snow, considerably slowing it down and negating its advantage.<br><br>Erymanthian boars aren't naturally occurring creatures, and were created as part of the Hecate Project, a bio-engineering program that applied mechanite replicators to already existing animals to mutate them into "magical" creatures.<br><br>Special Abilities: The tusks of the Erymanthian Boar can cause some serious wounds that keep bleeding and causing further injuries, making them VERY dangerous in close quarters. | Еримантійський кабан був гігантською, жахливою дикою свинею, яка мешкала на схилах гори Ерімантус, час від часу спустошуючи поля фермерів. Упіймання кабана було метою Четвертого подвигу Геракла. Щоб зловити кабана, Геракл загнав його в густий сніг, значно уповільнивши його просування і перевагу .<br><br>Еримантійські кабани не є природними істотами і були створені в рамках проекту Геката, біоінженерної програми, яка застосовувала реплікатори механітів до вже існуючих тварин, щоб мутувати їх в «чарівних» істот .<br><br>Особливі здатності: Клики еримантійського кабана можуть завдати кілька серйозних ран, які продовжують кровоточити і викликають подальші травми, що робить їх ДУЖЕ небезпечними в ближньому бою. |  |
